# Student training × refinement utility pilot

Experiment: `9f5b2f7daa83d0b62edb87318ef06c27e71c40563ed3c6b475d16ae4f78d9871`.

Scope: **matched_student_refinement_utility**. Source: `9a0212049f50fe838ae59cdf5450d9fb3a293172` / `71c65e48750f63dc24125d04b429bcf1938abd7e6b9019daac97f2fa88e4be53`.

These are frozen expert-FM utility measurements, not benchmark success. Diagnostic loss-selected packets are not deployed routers.

## Required student matrix

| Student | Measurement stage | Measured states | Status |
|---|---|---:|---|
| S_STD | main | 128 | partial_or_diagnostic |
| S_STAGE | main | 128 | partial_or_diagnostic |
| S_PACKET | main | 128 | partial_or_diagnostic |
| S_INIT | smoke | 8 | partial_or_diagnostic |

The primary matrix contains S_STD, S_STAGE and S_PACKET. S_INIT is the adopted teacher evaluated with compact contexts before any compact updates: its fitting-partition smoke checks numerical correctness and runtime only. It is excluded from primary plots, confirmation and router selection. Old S0 belongs to the earlier teacher study and is not part of this matrix.

This study declares 100,000 updates for each teacher-initialized student. Earlier numbered snapshots are development-only on the fitting partition; final confirmation uses each checkpoint's recorded primary endpoint. Existing studies without the extended schedule retain their original 30k endpoint. An explicitly configured S0 continuation uses its separately declared common endpoint. Initializations and training durations are never pooled in matched contrasts. S0 has a different prior training history.

Intervals resample episodes within tasks after averaging repeated draws/states. They do not measure training-seed robustness.

## Prevalence, null effects and all-negative pools

Candidate frequencies are averaged within states/episodes; decision-context frequencies are separate. Numerical sign repeatability is descriptive and is not statistical significance. Relative thresholds below are sensitivity choices, not established success thresholds.

Numerical gain deadband: `1e-06`.

## Hypotheses

| Hypothesis | Assessment | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| H1 | inconclusive | Primary matched endpoint panel incomplete; current results are diagnostic or partial |
| H2 | inconclusive | Primary matched endpoint panel incomplete; current results are diagnostic or partial |
| H3 | inconclusive | Primary matched endpoint panel incomplete; current results are diagnostic or partial |
| H4 | inconclusive | Optional equal-budget interaction panel not activated |
| H5 | inconclusive | Offline resolution losses cannot establish correct semantic choices; behavioral evidence required |
| H6 | inconclusive | Primary matched endpoint panel incomplete; current results are diagnostic or partial |
| H7 | inconclusive | Primary matched endpoint panel incomplete; current results are diagnostic or partial |
| H8 | inconclusive | Conditional learned-router/crossed adaptation stage not activated |

## Student training artifacts

Curves use only updates through the measured endpoint and are imported only from a hash-verified checkpoint directory. Realized strata include all available rank files and deduplicate repeated steps. Detailed curves and exposure counts are in `student_training.json`.

| Student | Artifact status | Endpoint | Logged updates | Strata |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| S_STD | available | 95000 | 95000 | 290 |
| S_STAGE | available | 95000 | 95000 | 292 |
| S_PACKET | available | 95000 | 95000 | 292 |

![Fixed-endpoint student learning curves](plots/student_learning.png)

## Eligibility and boundaries

Preselected states: 256; task counts and exclusions are retained in `manifest.json`.

Every selected diagnostic episode and the complete router-validation partition are excluded from new student/router fitting. Development and final partitions are reported separately. Existing teacher/S0 exposure is not claimed unseen.

The terminal/unavailable-input convention is unchanged. Full-H16 expert loss selects candidates; first-8 loss is secondary. Structural gripper masks are recorded independently of terminal padding.

| Reader / family / budget | Preselected states | Eligible | Ranking-eligible | Exclusion reasons |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| FLAT/immediate_audit/audit128 | 256 | 254 | 254 | Fewer than 128 causal leaves for B127-to-B128 audit: 2 |
| FLAT/packet/128 | 256 | 244 | 244 | Insufficient leaves for exact final packet budget: 2; No packet anchor at this background: 10 |
| FLAT/packet/64 | 256 | 244 | 244 | Insufficient leaves for exact final packet budget: 1; No packet anchor at this background: 11 |
| TREE/immediate_audit/audit128 | 256 | 254 | 254 | Fewer than 128 causal leaves for B127-to-B128 audit: 2 |
| TREE/packet/128 | 256 | 244 | 244 | Insufficient leaves for exact final packet budget: 2; No packet anchor at this background: 10 |
| TREE/packet/64 | 256 | 244 | 244 | Insufficient leaves for exact final packet budget: 1; No packet anchor at this background: 11 |

State-sampling exclusions: incomplete_available_action_horizon: 128.

| S_STD | 128 | 149000 | 19.040 | 310.101 | 3.713 | 10.815 | 0.000 | 0.209 |
| S_STAGE | 128 | 149000 | 22.367 | 339.696 | 3.267 | 10.786 | 1.823 | 0.209 |

| S_INIT | 8 | 8512 | 2.573 | 14.980 | 0.186 | 1.167 | 0.454 | 0.143 |

Frozen 3-student panel cost scenarios from the declared smoke: **0.07 / 0.33 / 1.15 serial hours** using observed minimum/median/maximum unit costs.

Observed min/median/max unit costs, not uncertainty intervals. Histories and storage may differ; parallel allocations may contend. Excludes startup, candidate construction, numerical controls, extra confirmation, generated actions, simulation and reports. No training duration is predicted.

## Artifacts

![Reproducible utility plots](plots/utility.png)

`analysis.json` preserves raw per-draw losses, selections, eligibility groupings and episode-aware statistics. `contexts/` stores fixed supports, moves, costs and exact-coverage identities. `measurements/` contains compressed draw arrays and checksums.

Regenerate without inference: `python -m stmem.inspection.refinement_utility --mode report --manifest PATH/manifest.json`.
